### [74] [Apriori-based Analysis of Learned Helplessness in Mathematics Tutoring: Behavioral Patterns by Level, Intervention, and Outcome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26237)
*John Paul P. Miranda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用Apriori算法分析数学辅导系统日志中习得性无助的行为交互模式，发现跳过问题（不使用提示）与未解决结果最相关，而坚持行为（不跳过）与成功解决相关。低习得性无助学生表现出更强的坚持-成功关联，高习得性无助学生则呈现更多回避模式。无干预组中坚持-成功关联最强，有干预组中跳过行为与未解决结果关联更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索数学辅导系统中学生习得性无助的行为交互模式，理解不同习得性无助水平、系统干预条件和问题解决结果下的行为差异。

Method: 使用Apriori关联规则挖掘算法，分析数学辅导系统日志中的行为交互序列，从习得性无助水平（低/高）、系统干预（有/无）和问题解决结果（解决/未解决）三个维度进行比较分析。

Result: (1) 整体数据中，跳过问题且不使用提示是与未解决结果最频繁的模式；不跳过等坚持行为整体不占主导。(2) 低习得性无助学生中问题解决与不跳过关联更强，提示使用与成功解决呈正相关；高习得性无助学生中跳过与未解决结果强关联，呈现更多回避模式。(3) 无干预组坚持-成功关联的提升度最高；有干预组中跳过行为与未解决结果的模式更强。(4) 不跳过在所有组中均与问题解决一致关联，跳过不使用提示则预测未解决结果。

Conclusion: 不同习得性无助水平和干预条件下的学生表现出差异化的行为模式，研究结果为针对性干预策略提供了实践建议。

Abstract: This study applied the Apriori algorithm to analyze behavioral interaction patterns associated with learned helplessness (LH) in mathematics tutoring system logs. Interaction data were examined across three dimensions: LH level (low vs. high), system-based intervention (with vs. without), and problem-solving outcomes (solved vs. unsolved). The analysis of the complete dataset showed that skipping problems without using hints was the most frequent pattern linked to unsolved outcomes, while persistence behaviors such as not skipping were less dominant overall. Comparisons by LH level showed that low-LH students had stronger links between problem solving and not skipping, as well as positive associations between hint use and solved outcomes. High-LH students showed more avoidance patterns, with skipping strongly tied to unsolved outcomes. In the comparison of system-based intervention conditions, students without intervention had the highest lift for persistence-success links, while the with-intervention group had stronger patterns involving skipping behaviors leading to unsolved outcomes. Outcome-specific analysis showed that not skipping was consistently associated with solved problems across all groups, while skipping without hints predicted unsolved outcomes. Practical implications and recommendations are discussed.

</details>


### [75] [DreamProver: Evolving Transferable Lemma Libraries via a Wake-Sleep Theorem-Proving Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26311)
*Youyuan Zhang,Jialiang Sun,Hangrui Bi,Chuqin Geng,Wenjie Ma,Zhaoyu Li,Xujie Si*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DreamProver是一个基于"唤醒-睡眠"程序归纳范式的智能体框架，通过迭代两阶段过程（唤醒阶段尝试证明定理并提议新引理，睡眠阶段对候选引理进行抽象、精炼和整合）自动发现可重用的引理库，从而提升形式化定理证明的成功率、证明简洁性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖固定的引理库导致适应性有限，要么为单个定理合成高度特化的中间引理而缺乏泛化性，因此需要一种能够自动发现可迁移、可重用引理的框架来弥补这一空白。

Method: 采用"唤醒-睡眠"两阶段迭代范式：唤醒阶段利用当前引理库证明训练集中的定理，同时提出新的候选引理；睡眠阶段对候选引理进行抽象、精炼和整合，以压缩和优化引理库。通过交替循环，逐步演化出一组紧凑、高层且可迁移的引理。

Result: 在多个数学基准测试上，DreamProver显著提升了证明成功率，同时生成了更简洁的证明，并降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: DreamProver通过自动发现可重用引理，有效提升了形式化定理证明的泛化能力和效率，证明了"唤醒-睡眠"归纳范式在引理发现任务中的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce DreamProver, an agentic framework that leverages a "wake-sleep" program induction paradigm to discover reusable lemmas for formal theorem proving. Existing approaches either rely on fixed lemma libraries, which limit adaptability, or synthesize highly specific intermediate lemmas tailored to individual theorems, thereby lacking generality. DreamProver addresses this gap through an iterative two-stage process. In the wake stage, DreamProver attempts to prove theorems from a training set using the current lemma library while proposing new candidate lemmas. In the "sleep" stage, it abstracts, refines, and consolidates these candidates to compress and optimize the library. Through this alternating cycle, DreamProver progressively evolves a compact set of high-level, transferable lemmas that can be effectively used to prove unseen theorems in related domains. Experimental results demonstrate that DreamProver substantially improves proof success rates across a diverse set of mathematical benchmarks, while also producing more concise proofs and reducing computational cost.

</details>


### [76] [AGEL-Comp: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Compositional Generalization in Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26522)
*Mahnoor Shahid,Hannes Rothe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AGEL-Comp，一种神经符号AI智能体架构，通过动态因果程序图、归纳逻辑编程引擎和神经定理证明器混合推理，解决LLM智能体在组合泛化中的系统性失败问题，在Retro Quest环境中表现优于纯LLM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体在组合泛化中存在系统性失败，限制了其在交互式环境中的鲁棒性，亟需一种能够显式构建可解释、组合结构化世界理解的智能体架构。

Method: AGEL-Comp包含三个核心创新：(1) 动态因果程序图(CPG)作为世界模型，以有向超图表示程序和因果知识；(2) 归纳逻辑编程(ILP)引擎从经验反馈中综合新的Horn子句，通过交互将符号知识具身化；(3) 混合推理核心中LLM提出候选子目标，由神经定理证明器(NTP)验证逻辑一致性，形成演绎-溯因学习循环。

Result: 在Retro Quest仿真环境中提出的评估协议下，AGEL模型在组合泛化场景中的性能明显优于纯LLM模型。

Conclusion: 该框架为构建具有显式、可解释、组合结构世界理解的智能体提供了原则性路径，展示了神经符号方法在组合泛化任务中的优势。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents exhibit systemic failures in compositional generalization, limiting their robustness in interactive environments. This work introduces AGEL-Comp, a neuro-symbolic AI agent architecture designed to address this challenge by grounding actions of the agent. AGEL-Comp integrates three core innovations: (1) a dynamic Causal Program Graph (CPG) as a world model, representing procedural and causal knowledge as a directed hypergraph; (2) an Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) engine that synthesizes new Horn clauses from experiential feedback, grounding symbolic knowledge through interaction; and (3) a hybrid reasoning core where an LLM proposes a set of candidate sub-goals that are verified for logical consistency by a Neural Theorem Prover (NTP). Together, these components operationalize a deduction--abduction learning cycle: enabling the agent to deduce plans and abductively expand its symbolic world model, while a neural adaptation phase keeps its reasoning engine aligned with new knowledge. We propose an evaluation protocol within the \texttt{Retro Quest} simulation environment to probe for compositional generalization scenarios to evaluate our AGEL agent. Our findings clearly indicate the better performance of our AGEL model over pure LLM-based models. Our framework presents a principled path toward agents that build an explicit, interpretable, and compositionally structured understanding of their world.

</details>


### [77] [Benchmarking the Safety of Large Language Models for Robotic Health Attendant Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26577)
*Mahiro Nakao,Kazuhiro Takemoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了72个大语言模型在机器人健康护理员场景中的安全性，发现平均违规率高达54.4%，开源模型（中位72.8%）远不如闭源模型（中位23.7%）安全，且医疗领域微调未带来显著安全提升，提示安全性应作为此类系统开发的首要标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被部署为机器人健康护理员的控制组件，但其在此场景下的安全性尚未得到充分表征，因此亟需系统性评估。

Method: 构建了涵盖AMA医学伦理原则九类禁止行为的270条有害指令数据集，在基于机器人健康护理员框架的仿真环境中评估了72个LLM的违规率。

Result: 模型平均违规率54.4%，过半模型超50%；设备操纵、紧急延误等表面合理的指令更难拒绝；模型规模和发布日期是开源模型安全性的主要决定因素；闭源模型远优于开源模型（中位23.7% vs 72.8%）；医疗领域微调无显著安全提升；提示防御策略仅带来微弱改善。

Conclusion: 安全性评估必须作为LLM在机器人健康护理员场景中开发和部署的一级标准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly considered for deployment as the control component of robotic health attendants, yet their safety in this context remains poorly characterized. We introduce a dataset of 270 harmful instructions spanning nine prohibited behavior categories grounded in the American Medical Association Principles of Medical Ethics, and use it to evaluate 72 LLMs in a simulation environment based on the Robotic Health Attendant framework. The mean violation rate across all models was 54.4\%, with more than half exceeding 50\%, and violation rates varied substantially across behavior categories, with superficially plausible instructions such as device manipulation and emergency delay proving harder to refuse than overtly destructive ones. Model size and release date were the primary determinants of safety performance among open-weight models, and proprietary models were substantially safer than open-weight counterparts (median 23.7\% versus 72.8\%). Medical domain fine-tuning conferred no significant overall safety benefit, and a prompt-based defense strategy produced only a modest reduction in violation rates among the least safe models, leaving absolute violation rates at levels that would preclude safe clinical deployment. These findings demonstrate that safety evaluation must be treated as a first-class criterion in the development and deployment of LLMs for robotic health attendants.

</details>


### [78] [Human-in-the-Loop Benchmarking of Heterogeneous LLMs for Automated Competency Assessment in Secondary Level Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26607)
*Jatin Bhusal,Nancy Mahatha,Aayush Acharya,Raunak Regmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“人在回路中”的基准测试框架，用于评估多个LLM在自动化中学数学评估中的有效性。基于尼泊尔十年级数学课程，创建了多维评估标准。测试了四个模型（Eagle-8B, Orion-70B, Nova, Lyra），发现存在显著的“架构兼容性差距”：基于Gemini的稀疏MoE模型达到“公平一致”（kappa_w~0.38），而更大的Orion（70B）模型却显示“无一致性”（kappa_w=-0.0261），表明在受评分标准约束的任务中，架构对指令约束的遵循比原始参数规模更重要。结论是LLM尚不适合自主认证，但在“人在回路中”框架下可作为高价值辅助工具进行初步证据提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于能力的教育（CBE）在全球范围内逐渐普及，但从基于分数的评估转向定性能力图谱对教育者而言是一项手动挑战。本文旨在通过“人在回路中”的基准测试框架，解决这一瓶颈问题，评估多个大语言模型在自动化中学数学评估中的有效性。

Method: 基于尼泊尔十年级数学课程，创建了涵盖四个主题和四项跨领域能力（理解、知识、操作流畅性、行为与相关性）的多维评分标准。采用多供应商集成方法，包含开源模型（Eagle-8B, Orion-70B）和专有前沿模型（Nova, Lyra），以两位高级数学教师定义的地面真值为基准（kappa_w=0.8652）进行评测。

Result: 存在显著的“架构兼容性差距”：Gemini-based 稀疏MoE模型达到“公平一致”（kappa_w~0.38），而更大的Orion（70B）模型显示“无一致性”（kappa_w=-0.0261）。架构对指令约束的遵循比原始参数规模更重要。

Conclusion: LLM尚不适合自主认证，但在“人在回路中”框架下可作为高价值辅助工具进行初步证据提取。

Abstract: As Competency-Based Education (CBE) is gaining traction around the world, the shift from marks-based assessment to qualitative competency mapping is a manual challenge for educators. This paper tackles the bottleneck issue by suggesting a "Human-in-the-Loop" benchmarking framework to assess the effectiveness of multiple LLMs in automating secondary-level mathematics assessment. Based on the Grade 10 Optional Mathematics curriculum in Nepal, we created a multi-dimensional rubric for four topics and four cross-cutting competencies: Comprehension, Knowledge, Operational Fluency, and Behavior and Correlation.
  The multi-provider ensemble, consisted of open-weight models -- Eagle (Llama 3.1-8B) and Orion (Llama 3.3-70B) -- and proprietary frontier models Nova (Gemini 2.5 Flash) and Lyra (Gemini 3 Pro), was benchmarked against a ground truth defined by two senior mathematics faculty members (kappa_w = 0.8652). The findings show a marked "Architecture-compatibility gap". Although the Gemini-based Mixture-of-Experts (Sparse MoE) models achieved "Fair Agreement" (kappa_w ~ 0.38), the larger Orion (70B) model exhibited "No Agreement" (kappa_w = -0.0261), suggesting that architectural compliance with instruction constraints outweighs the scale of raw parameters in rubric-constrained tasks. We conclude that while LLMs are not yet suitable for autonomous certification, they provide high-value assistive support for preliminary evidence extraction within a "Human-in-the-Loop" framework.

</details>


### [79] [When to Vote, When to Rewrite: Disagreement-Guided Strategy Routing for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26644)
*Zhimin Lin,Yixin Ji,Jinpeng Li,Yu Luo,Dong Li,Junhua Fang,Juntao Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于输出分歧的训练无关测试时缩放框架，将实例级别的策略路由问题替代单一策略内增加计算，动态选择不同缩放策略（轻量推理、多数投票、重写重构），在7个数学基准上提升3%-7%准确率并降低采样成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时缩放方法（重复采样、自纠错、树搜索）在困难问题上收益递减，且缺乏针对实例难度的自适应策略选择机制。

Method: 将测试时缩放建模为实例级路由问题：利用输出分歧作为难度和正确性的信号，对一致性高的实例用轻量推理，中等分歧用多数投票，高度歧义用重写重构。

Result: 在7个数学基准和3个模型上，准确率提升3%-7%，且采样成本低于现有方法。

Conclusion: 输出分歧可作为有效的策略选择信号，基于路由的测试时缩放比单一策略内增加计算更高效。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on mathematical reasoning tasks but remain unreliable on challenging instances. Existing test-time scaling methods, such as repeated sampling, self-correction, and tree search, improve performance at the cost of increased computation, yet often exhibit diminishing returns on hard problems. We observe that output disagreement is strongly correlated with instance difficulty and prediction correctness, providing a useful signal for guiding instance-level strategy selection at test time. Based on this insight, we propose a training-free framework that formulates test-time scaling as an instance-level routing problem, rather than allocating more computation within a single strategy, dynamically selecting among different scaling strategies based on output disagreement. The framework applies lightweight resolution for consistent cases, majority voting for moderate disagreement, and rewriting-based reformulation for highly ambiguous instances. Experiments on seven mathematical benchmarks and three models show that our method improves accuracy by 3% - 7% while reducing sampling cost compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [80] [SciHorizon-DataEVA: An Agentic System for AI-Readiness Evaluation of Heterogeneous Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26645)
*Dianyu Liu,Chuan Qin,Xi Chen,Xiaohan Li,Wenxi Xu,Yuyang Wang,Xin Chen,Yuanchun Zhou,Hengshu Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciHorizon-DataEVA，一个用于对异构科学数据进行可扩展的AI就绪性评估的智能体系统，包括Sci-TQA2原则（四个维度）和基于层级多智能体的工作流Sci-TQA2-Eval。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据的AI就绪性从根本上限制了AI4Science模型的有效性，但目前缺乏可扩展且系统的评估机制。

Method: 提出Sci-TQA2原则（治理可信度、数据质量、AI兼容性、科学适应性四个维度）并分解为可测量的原子元素；开发Sci-TQA2-Eval层级多智能体评估方法，包含轻量级数据集分析、适用性感知度量激活、知识增强规划，以及带有验证和自校正的自适应工具中心评估机制。

Result: 在跨多个领域的科学数据集上进行了广泛实验，证明了SciHorizon-DataEVA在原则性AI就绪性评估方面的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: SciHorizon-DataEVA能够对异构科学数据进行可扩展、可靠的AI就绪性评估，填补了该领域的空白。

Abstract: AI-for-Science (AI4Science) is increasingly transforming scientific discovery by embedding machine learning models into prediction, simulation, and hypothesis generation workflows across domains. However, the effectiveness of these models is fundamentally constrained by the AI-readiness of scientific data, for which no scalable and systematic evaluation mechanism currently exists. In this work, we propose SciHorizon-DataEVA, a novel agentic system to scalable AI-readiness evaluation of heterogeneous scientific data. At the evaluation-criteria level, we introduce the Sci-TQA2 principles, which organize AI-readiness into four complementary dimensions: Governance Trustworthiness, Data Quality, AI Compatibility, and Scientific Adaptability. Each dimension is decomposed into measurable atomic elements that enable fine-grained and executable assessment. To operationalize these principles at scale, we develop Sci-TQA2-Eval, a hierarchical multi-agent evaluation approach orchestrated through a directed, cyclic workflow. Our Sci-TQA2-Eval dynamically constructs dataset-aware evaluation specifications by combining lightweight dataset profiling, applicability-aware metric activation, and knowledge-augmented planning grounded in domain constraints and dataset-paper signals. These specifications are executed through an adaptive, tool-centric evaluation mechanism with built-in verification and self-correction, enabling scalable and reliable assessment across heterogeneous scientific data. Extensive experiments on scientific datasets spanning multiple domains demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of SciHorizon-DataEVA for principled AI-readiness evaluation.

</details>


### [81] [FutureWorld: A Live Environment for Training Predictive Agents with Real-World Outcome Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26733)
*Zhixin Han,Yanzhi Zhang,Chuyang Wei,Maohang Gao,Xiawei Yue,Kefei Chen,Yu Zhuang,Haoxiang Guan,Jiyan He,Jian Li,Yitong Duan,Yu Shi,Mengting Hu,Shuxin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FutureWorld，一个基于实时未来预测的智能体强化学习环境，通过预测-结果实现-参数更新的闭环训练流程，在连续多天的训练中验证了有效性，并建立了当前前沿智能体系统的性能基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有未来预测研究分散在不同方向，缺乏统一的训练环境；而实时未来预测能提供大量基于真实事件的预测问题且防止答案泄露，适合作为智能体持续学习的训练场景。

Method: 构建FutureWorld环境，实现预测、结果实现与参数更新的闭环训练；选取三个开源基座模型进行连续多天训练；同时基于该环境建立每日基准评测集。

Result: 连续多天的训练表明该训练范式有效；在所建基准上评估了多个前沿智能体系统，建立了性能基线。

Conclusion: FutureWorld作为实时未来预测的智能体强化学习环境，能有效驱动智能体在真实世界事件中持续学习，为未来预测研究提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Live future prediction refers to the task of making predictions about real-world events before they unfold. This task is increasingly studied using large language model-based agent systems, and it is important for building agents that can continually learn from real-world. Just as interactive environments have often driven progress in agents, advancing live future prediction naturally motivates viewing it as a learning environment. Prior works have explored future prediction from several different parts, but have generally not framed it as a unified learning environment. This task is appealing for learning because it can provide a large number of prediction questions grounded in diverse real-world events, while preventing answer leakage. To leverage the advantages of live future prediction, we present FutureWorld, a live agentic reinforcement learning environment that closes the training loop between prediction, outcome realization, and parameters update. In our environment, we take three open-source base models and train them for consecutive days. The results show that training is effective. Furthermore, we build a daily benchmark based on the environment and evaluate several frontier agents on it to establish performance baselines for current agent systems.

</details>


### [82] [Bian Que: An Agentic Framework with Flexible Skill Arrangement for Online System Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26805)
*Bochao Liu,Zhipeng Qian,Yang Zhao,Xinyuan Jiang,Zihan Liang,Yufei Ma,Junpeng Zhuang,Ben Chen,Shuo Yang,Hongen Wan,Yao Wu,Chenyi Lei,Xiao Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Bian Que 是一个面向大规模在线引擎系统运维的智能体框架，通过统一运维范式、灵活技能编排和自进化机制，在快手电商搜索引擎部署后实现了告警量减少75%、根因分析准确率80%、平均解决时间降低50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模在线引擎系统（搜索、推荐、广告）的运维依赖大量人力进行发布监控、告警响应和根因分析。LLM智能体虽然适合这些任务，但瓶颈不在于推理能力，而在于编排能力——如何为每个运维事件选择相关数据（指标、日志、变更事件）和适用的运维知识（手册规则和实践经验）。不加区分地输入所有信号会导致信息稀释和幻觉，而手动编排事件到（数据、知识）的映射在每天数十次发布的情况下是不可行的。

Method: 提出Bian Que框架，包含三项关键技术：(i) 统一运维范式——将日常运维抽象为三种规范模式：发布拦截、主动巡检、告警根因分析；(ii) 灵活技能编排——每个Skill指定在给定业务模块上下文中应检索哪些数据和知识，可由LLM自动生成更新或通过自然语言指令由值班工程师迭代优化；(iii) 统一自进化机制——一个修正信号驱动两条并行路径：案例记忆到知识的蒸馏以及针对性的Skill改进。

Result: 在快手电商搜索引擎上部署后，告警量减少75%，根因分析准确率达80%，平均解决时间降低超过50%。离线评估通过率达99.0%。

Conclusion: Bian Que通过将运维范式规范化为三种模式、灵活的技能自动编排与自进化机制，有效解决了LLM智能体在大规模在线系统运维中的编排瓶颈问题，显著提升了运维效率。

Abstract: Operating and maintaining (O&M) large-scale online engine systems (search, recommendation, advertising) demands substantial human effort for release monitoring, alert response, and root cause analysis. While LLM-based agents are a natural fit for these tasks, the deployment bottleneck is not reasoning capability but orchestration: selecting, for each operational event, the relevant data (metrics, logs, change events) and the applicable operational knowledge (handbook rules and practitioner experience). Feeding all signals indiscriminately causes dilution and hallucination, while manually curating the event-to-(data, knowledge) mapping is intractable under dozens of daily releases. We present Bian Que, an agentic framework with three contributions: (i) a \emph{unified operational paradigm} abstracting day-to-day O&M into three canonical patterns: release interception, proactive inspection, and alert root cause analysis; (ii) \emph{Flexible Skill Arrangement}, where each Skill specifies which data and knowledge to retrieve for a given business-module context and can be automatically generated and updated by LLMs or iteratively refined through natural-language instructions from on-call engineers; (iii) a \emph{unified self-evolving mechanism} in which one correction signal drives two parallel pathways, case-memory-to-knowledge distillation and targeted Skill refinement. Deployed on the e-commerce search engine of KuaiShou, the major short-video platform in China, Bian Que reduces alert volume by 75%, achieves 80% root-cause analysis accuracy, and cuts mean time to resolution by over 50%. Our framework achieves 99.0% pass rate on offline evaluations. Our code is available at https://github.com/benchen4395/BianQue_Assistant.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [FalconApp: Rapid iPhone Deployment of End-to-End Perception via Automatically Labeled Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25949)
*Yan Miao,Will Shen,Sayan Mitra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FalconApp是一款iPhone应用，通过端到端流水线将手持拍摄的物体视频转化为可用于掩码检测和6-DoF位姿估计的感知模块，核心在于快速移动部署流水线结合逼真自动标注工作流，平均每物体约20分钟合成数据生成与训练，iPhone端到端延迟约30ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人感知依赖大规模标注数据，但真实数据集需要大量人工标注且耗时，因此需要一种快速、自动化的方法来生成带标注的训练数据并部署感知模型。

Method: 用户用iPhone拍摄物体视频，FalconApp重建可编辑的GSplat资产，将其与多样化逼真背景合成，渲染带真实掩码和位姿的合成图像，训练感知模块，最后部署回iPhone前端。

Result: 在5个刚性物体上的实验表明：平均每物体约20分钟合成数据生成与训练，iPhone端到端延迟约30ms，在4/5个物体上的位姿精度优于PnP基线。

Conclusion: FalconApp展示了利用手机端快速生成可部署感知模型的可行性，有效降低了机器人感知对人工标注的依赖。

Abstract: Reliable perception for robotics depends on large-scale labeled data, yet real-world datasets rely on heavy manual annotation and are time-consuming to produce. We present FalconApp, an iPhone app with an end-to-end frontend-backend pipeline that turns a short handheld capture of a rigid object into a perception module for mask detection and 6-DoF pose estimation. Our core contribution is a rapid mobile deployment pipeline paired with a photorealistic auto-labeling workflow: from a user-captured video of an object, FalconApp reconstructs an editable GSplat asset, composites it with diverse photorealistic backgrounds, renders synthetic images with ground-truth masks and poses, trains the perception module, and deploys it back to the iPhone frontend. Experiments across five rigid objects with diverse geometry and appearance show that FalconApp produces usable perception models with about 20 minutes of synthetic-data generation and training per object on average, around 30 ms end-to-end on-device latency on iPhone, and better overall pose accuracy than a PnP baseline on 4 / 5 objects in both simulation and real-world evaluation.

</details>


### [84] [A Scaled Three-Vehicle Platooning Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25963)
*Kaiyue Lu,Qiaoxuan Zhang,Yukun Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IMRL实验室开发的缩比多车平台，用于自动驾驶队列研究，包含一辆人控领车和两辆自主跟随车，重点研究队列稳定性和路径跟踪控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆队列在动态 maneuvers 中保持稳定路径跟踪是关键挑战，尤其是前车横向偏差和航向扰动向后传播影响队列稳定性，需要鲁棒的纵横向控制系统。

Method: 开发缩比多车实验平台，包含1辆人控领车和2辆自主跟随车；采用领车-跟随车协调控制架构；支持人机协同控制与可重复实验。

Result: 该平台相比全尺寸实车测试更安全、成本更低、更灵活，相比纯仿真评估具有更强的物理真实性，可用于快速原型验证、控制器评估和多智能体自主研究。

Conclusion: 缩比平台在安全、成本、灵活性方面具有优势，同时保持了较高的物理真实性，是介于全尺寸实验与纯仿真之间的有效中间方案。

Abstract: Vehicle platooning has attracted increasing attention as a promising approach to improve traffic efficiency, energy consumption, and roadway safety through coordinated multi-vehicle operation. A key challenge in platooning lies in maintaining stable and accurate path tracking during dynamic maneuvers such as lane changes, where lateral deviations and heading disturbances generated by the lead vehicle may propagate downstream to following vehicles. Robust longitudinal and lateral control systems are therefore essential not only for individual vehicle tracking performance, but also for overall platoon stability. For experimental studies, the Intelligent Mobility and Robotics Lab (IMRL) develops a scaled multi-vehicle platform for autonomous platooning research, with a particular emphasis on cooperative control and human-in-the-loop autonomy. This platform consists of one human-operable lead vehicle and two autonomous followers, enabling controlled and repeatable experiments on leader-follower coordination. Compared with full-scale field testing, this scaled platform offers a safer, lower-cost, and more flexible environment for rapid prototyping, controller validation, and multi-agent autonomy studies, while providing stronger physical realism than purely simulation-based evaluations.

</details>


### [85] [Multi-Periodogram Velocity Estimation with Irregular Reference Signals for Robot-Aided ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25974)
*Yi Geng,Pan Cao,Ting Zeng,Yongqian Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向机器人辅助集成感知与通信（ISAC）场景的多周期图速度估计算法，该算法利用5G/6G非规则参考信号（RS）的时域结构分解特性，在不增加专用感知RS或修改3GPP标准的前提下，实现了比传统周期图处理低3dB SNR的检测门限（10%漏检率）并降低51%虚警率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ISAC速度估计方法依赖专用感知参考信号或3GPP协议修改，而移动机器人作为感知节点只能机会性复用非规则的5G/6G参考信号，导致传统周期图处理方法在低SNR下性能严重退化。

Method: 首先证明非规则时域参考信号诱导的速度谱可分解为周期峰值分量和幅度整形（加权）分量；基于该结构，提出多周期图速度估计算法，通过融合多个周期图实现标准兼容的速度估计。

Result: 与传统周期图方法相比，所提算法在10%漏检率处获得3dB SNR增益，虚警率降低51%，显著提升低SNR鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法无需新增感知专用RS或修改3GPP标准，为机器人辅助ISAC系统提供了标准兼容、低SNR鲁棒的速度估计方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses velocity estimation within robot-aided integrated sensing and communications (ISAC), where mobile robots act as sensing nodes but can only opportunistically reuse irregular 5G/6G reference signals (RSs). We show that the velocity profile induced by such irregular time-domain patterns can be decomposed into a periodic-peak component and an amplitude-shaping (weighting) component. Leveraging this structure, we propose a multi-periodogram velocity estimation algorithm that is standard-compliant and does not require new sensing-dedicated RSs or 3GPP modifications. Simulation results demonstrate that, compared with conventional periodogram processing, the proposed method improves low-SNR robustness by achieving a 3 dB SNR gain at the 10% missed-detection rate and reducing false alarms by 51%.

</details>


### [86] [FlowS: One-Step Motion Prediction via Local Transport Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26065)
*Leandro Di Bella,Adrian Munteanu,Bruno Cornelis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowS提出了一种局部传输条件化（local transport conditioning）策略，通过场景条件化的学习先验和步骤一致性位移场，使得单步扩散模型生成运动预测在保持高精度和多模态多样性的同时，满足严格延迟约束，在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上达到SOTA性能（Soft mAP 0.4804, mAP 0.4703, 75 FPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式运动预测方法（扩散模型）虽然能同时满足高精度和多模态多样性，但需要数十到数百步去噪推理，违反了安全关键型自动驾驶对严格延迟的要求。作者旨在解决精度、多模态与低延迟三者间的矛盾。

Method: 1) 提出局部传输条件化概念：当底层传输问题是局部的（即基础分布已经接近未来轨迹），单步Euler积分即可达到高精度；2) 在线场景条件化学习先验：为每个智能体生成K个校准锚点轨迹，将模式发现转化为局部校正；3) 步骤一致性位移场：强制半群自一致性，保证单步继承多步精度；4) 将位移场锚定在沿直线路径的学习先验上，得到稳定低方差训练目标，克服弯曲扩散路径上高方差bootstrap问题。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上，FlowS以单步推理实现了Soft mAP 0.4804和mAP 0.4703（集成版本），推理速度达75 FPS，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 局部传输条件化使得单步生成式运动预测在安全关键型自动驾驶中变得实用，成功弥合了生成模型精度、多样性与实时性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Generative motion prediction must satisfy three simultaneous requirements for real-world autonomy: high accuracy, diverse multimodal futures, and strictly bounded latency. Diffusion models meet the first two but violate the third, requiring tens to hundreds of denoising steps. We identify a conditioning strategy that resolves this tension: \textit{single-step integration is accurate when the underlying transport problem is local}. A model that must both discover the correct behavioral mode and traverse a long displacement in one step accumulates large discretization errors; conditioning the base distribution to lie near plausible futures reduces the problem to short-range refinement, the regime where a single Euler step suffices. We instantiate this \emph{local transport conditioning} in FlowS, a conditional flow matching framework with two mechanisms. First, an online, scene-conditioned learned prior emits $K$ calibrated anchor trajectories per agent, each already near a plausible future, converting mode discovery into local correction. Second, a step-consistent displacement field enforces semigroup self-consistency, guaranteeing that a single step inherits multi-step accuracy. Crucially, anchoring this field at learned priors along straight-line paths yields a {stable, low-variance} training target, unlike prior self-consistency methods that suffer from {high-variance bootstrap} signals on curved diffusion paths. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, FlowS achieves state-of-the-art Soft mAP {(0.4804) and mAP (0.4703) with ensemble at 75\,FPS} with single-step inference, demonstrating that local transport conditioning makes one-step generative motion prediction practical for safety-critical autonomy. Code and pretrained models will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [87] [Lights Out: A Nighttime UAV Localization Framework Using Thermal Imagery and Semantic 3D Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26201)
*Ryan Allen,Melissa Greeff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语义重投影框架，通过将夜间热成像分割结果与日间RGB数据构建的语义3D地图对齐，实现了GNSS拒止环境下无人机在夜间的全局定位。在6.5公里真实飞行轨迹上，系统达到2.18米的偏差校正RMSE2D，证明了仅使用热成像进行夜间无人机定位的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS拒止的夜间条件下，无人机可靠备份定位仍是一个开放挑战，主要由于日间RGB地图与夜间热成像之间存在巨大的模态差异。

Method: 提出语义重投影框架：将分割后的热观测与日间RGB数据构建的全局语义标注3D地图对齐；在共享语义域中通过对称双向重投影目标函数进行定位，并引入混淆感知权重以增强对分割不确定性的鲁棒性。

Result: 在城市和半结构化环境的6.5公里夜间真实无人机飞行轨迹上，相对于RTK GNSS真值，系统偏差校正RMSE2D为2.18米，中值RMSE2D为1.52米。定位性能与语义边缘证据的可用性强相关，大误差事件在空间上集中于语义模糊区域。

Conclusion: 语义重投影为仅使用热成像进行夜间无人机全局定位提供了一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Reliable backup localization for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) operating in GNSS-denied nighttime conditions remains an open challenge due to the severe modality gap between daytime RGB maps and nighttime thermal imagery. This work presents a semantic reprojection framework for map-relative nighttime UAV localization by aligning segmented thermal observations with a globally referenced, semantically labeled 3D map constructed from daytime RGB data. Rather than relying on appearance-based correspondence, localization is formulated in a shared semantic domain and solved via a symmetric bidirectional reprojection objective with confusion-aware weighting to improve robustness under segmentation uncertainty. The approach is evaluated offline across 6.5 km of nighttime, real-world UAV flight trajectories in urban and semi-structured environments. Relative to RTK GNSS ground truth, the system achieves a bias-corrected RMSE2D of 2.18 m and a median RMSE2D of 1.52 m. Results show that localization performance is strongly correlated with the availability of semantic edge evidence and that large-error events are spatially localized to semantically ambiguous areas rather than uniformly distributed. These findings indicate that semantic reprojection offers a promising pathway toward globally referenced nighttime UAV localization using thermal imagery alone.

</details>


### [88] [2D and 3D Grasp Planners for the GET Asymmetrical Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26212)
*Andrew Goldberg,Ethan Ransing,Anton Kourakin,Cael Magner,Edward H. Adelson,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种针对非对称GET夹爪的抓取规划器：基于单视图RGB-D图像和Ferrari-Canny度量的快速规划器GET-2D-1.0（683ms），以及基于网格和光线追踪的GET-3D-1.0（17s）。物理实验表明，GET-2D-1.0相比基线在提升成功率、抗抖动和抗力性能上提升超40%；GET-3D-1.0略有提升但计算成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非对称GET夹爪，开发快速且有效的抓取规划方法，解决现有方法在非对称夹爪上的适用性和效率问题。

Method: 提出两种方法：GET-2D-1.0基于单视角RGB-D图像，采用Ferrari-Canny度量与新型采样策略；GET-3D-1.0基于3D网格模型和光线追踪技术。

Result: GET-2D-1.0相比包围盒基线在提升成功率、抗抖动和抗力性能上提升超40%；GET-3D-1.0在提升成功率和抗抖动上略有改进，但规划时间长达17秒（GET-2D-1.0仅683ms）。

Conclusion: GET-2D-1.0在效率与性能间取得了良好平衡，适合实时抓取任务；GET-3D-1.0精度更高但计算成本过高，实际应用受限。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce GET-2D-1.0, a fast grasp planner for the GET asymmetrical gripper that operates from a single-view RGB-D image, using the Ferrari-Canny metric and a novel sampling strategy, and GET-3D-1.0, a mesh-based method using a 3D gripper model and ray-tracing. We evaluate both grasp planners against baselines with physical experiments, which suggest that GET-2D-1.0 can improve over a bounding box baseline by over 40% in lift success, shake survival, and force resistance. Experiments with GET-3D-1.0 suggest slight improvement compared to GET-2D-1.0 on lift success and shake survival, but are more computationally expensive, averaging 17 seconds of planning compared to 683 ms for GET-2D-1.0.

</details>


### [89] [Split over $n$ resource sharing problem: Are fewer capable agents better than many simpler ones?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26374)
*Karthik Soma,Mohamed S. Talamali,Genki Miyauchi,Giovanni Beltrame,Heiko Hamann,Roderich Gross*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体系统中有限资源应集中还是分散的问题，通过数学建模和仿真分析，发现资源分配方式对系统性能有显著影响，具体取决于智能体速度与尺寸的缩放关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统中，资源是有限的，需要探讨将资源集中到少数能力强的智能体还是分散到多个简单智能体更优，以指导系统设计。

Method: 形式化定义了n个智能体均分共享资源的问题，以多智能体覆盖为案例，假设智能体盘形覆盖区域面积与1/n成比例，进行了理论分析和计算机仿真。

Result: 初始覆盖率随n增加而提高；若智能体速度与其半径成比例减小，各组规模性能相当；若速度与其覆盖面积成比例减小，单个智能体最优；资源分散增加个体故障率。

Conclusion: 模型和发现有助于识别最优的分布式程度，为资源约束下多智能体系统的设计提供指导。

Abstract: In multi-agent systems, should limited resources be concentrated into a few capable agents or distributed among many simpler ones? This work formulates the split over $n$ resource sharing problem where a group of $n$ agents equally shares a common resource (e.g., monetary budget, computational resources, physical size). We present a case study in multi-agent coverage where the area of the disk-shaped footprint of agents scales as $1/n$. A formal analysis reveals that the initial coverage rate grows with $n$. However, if the speed of agents decreases proportionally with their radii, groups of all sizes perform equally well, whereas if it decreases proportionally with their footprints, a single agent performs best. We also present computer simulations in which resource splitting increases the failure rates of individual agents. The models and findings help identify optimal distributiveness levels and inform the design of multi-agent systems under resource constraints.

</details>


### [90] [Reactive Motion Generation via Phase-varying Neural Potential Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26450)
*Ahmet Tekden,Dimitrios Kanoulas,Aude Billard,Yasemin Bekiroglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Phase-varying Neural Potential Functions (PNPF)框架，通过将势函数与状态驱动的相位变量条件化，解决动态系统学习中轨迹交叉点的歧义问题，实现稳定、鲁棒的机器人操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态系统方法在轨迹交叉点处存在歧义：二阶动力学模型依赖速度进行方向区分，对扰动敏感且可能在相同位置-速度对下失败；基于相位的方法依赖开环时间/相位变量，扰动后恢复能力受限。

Method: 提出PNPF框架，将势函数（potential function）条件化在一个由状态进程直接估计的相位变量上（而非开环时间输入），该相位变量允许系统处理状态重访，同时学习到的势函数生成局部向量场以实现反应式且稳定的控制。

Result: 在点对点、周期性及完整6D运动任务上有效泛化，在含交叉点的轨迹任务上超越现有基线，并在实时机器人操控中对外部扰动展现出鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: PNPF通过状态驱动的相位变量结合势函数学习，有效解决了轨迹交叉点处的运动歧义问题，兼顾了稳定性与扰动恢复能力。

Abstract: Dynamical systems (DS) methods for Learning-from-Demonstration (LfD) provide stable, continuous policies from few demonstrations. First-order dynamical systems (DS) are effective for many point-to-point and periodic tasks, as long as a unique velocity is defined for each state. For tasks with intersections (e.g., drawing an "8"), extensions such as second-order dynamics or phase variables are often used. However, by incorporating velocity, second-order models become sensitive to disturbances near intersections, as velocity is used to disambiguate motion direction. Moreover, this disambiguation may fail when nearly identical position-velocity pairs correspond to different onward motions. In contrast, phase-based methods rely on open-loop time or phase variables, which limit their ability to recover after perturbations. We introduce Phase-varying Neural Potential Functions (PNPF), an LfD framework that conditions a potential function on a phase variable which is estimated directly from state progression, rather than on open-loop temporal inputs. This phase variable allows the system to handle state revisits, while the learned potential function generates local vector fields for reactive and stable control. PNPF generalizes effectively across point-to-point, periodic, and full 6D motion tasks, outperforms existing baselines on trajectories with intersections, and demonstrates robust performance in real-time robotic manipulation under external disturbances.

</details>


### [91] [Alter-Art: Exploring Embodied Artistic Creation through a Robot Avatar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26473)
*Do Won Park,Samuele Bordini,Giorgio Grioli,Manuel G. Catalano,Antonio Bicchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Alter-Art范式，通过机器人化身Alter-Ego让艺术家以第一人称体验远程临场创作，在舞蹈、戏剧和绘画领域验证了具身化对创作主体性、身份认同和环境交互的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人艺术框架将机器人视为自主创作者或协作伙伴，在人与机器之间存在鸿沟；本文旨在填补这一空白，让艺术家通过远程具身化真正"成为"机器人，超越物理限制重塑创作身份。

Method: 设计机器人化身Alter-Ego，集成沉浸式遥操作与柔顺驱动；在舞蹈、戏剧和绘画三个艺术领域开展实践；通过定性艺术反馈分析具身化对创作主体性、身份及环境交互的影响。

Result: 艺术家能快速建立对机器人身体的临场感；机器人的物理约束以不同方式影响各艺术领域的创作过程；具身化体验在不同艺术门类中呈现差异化表现。

Conclusion: 具身化是社会机器人设计中的核心原则；Alter-Art范式拓展了远程临在与可及性艺术表达的可能性，为人类与机器的新型创作关系提供了实践路径。

Abstract: As with every emerging technology, new tools in the hands of artists reshape the nature of artwork creation. Current frameworks for robotics in arts deploy the robot as an autonomous creator or a collaborator, thus leaving a certain gap between the human artist and the machine. Now, we stand at the dawn of an era where artists can escape physical limitations and reshape their creative identity by inhabiting an alternative body. This new paradigm allows artists not only to command a robot remotely, but also to {\it be} a robot, to see and feel through it, experiencing a new embodied reality. Unlike virtual reality, where art is created in a digital dimension, in this case art creation is still firmly grounded in the material world: clay molded by mechanical hands, paint swept across a canvas or gestures performed on a physical stage alongside human actors. Through the robot avatar Alter-Ego, we explore the Alter-Art paradigm in dance, theater, and painting; it integrates immersive teleoperation and compliant actuation to enable a first-person creative experience. Analyzing qualitative artistic feedback, we investigate how embodiment shapes creative agency, identity and interaction with the environment. Our findings suggest that artists rapidly develop a sense of presence within the robotic body. The robot's physical constraints influence the creative process, manifesting differently across artistic domains. We highlight embodiment as a central design principle, contributing to social robotics and expanding the possibilities for telepresence and accessible artistic expression.

</details>


### [92] [HiPAN: Hierarchical Posture-Adaptive Navigation for Quadruped Robots in Unstructured 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26504)
*Jeil Jeong,Minsung Yoon,Seokryun Choi,Heechan Shin,Taegeun Yang,Sung-eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HiPAN，一种基于深度图像的分层后肢自适应导航框架，通过高层策略生成导航指令、低层控制器执行运动，并结合路径引导的课程学习实现长程导航，在仿真和真实实验中均优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统顺序建图-规划方法存在感知误差累积和计算开销大的问题，难以在资源受限平台上部署；同时四足机器人在非结构3D环境中面临目标导向运动、局部极小值逃脱和高度受限空间穿越等挑战。

Method: HiPAN采用分层设计：高层策略生成平面速度和身体姿态等导航指令，低层后肢自适应控制器执行运动；引入路径引导的课程学习，渐进式地从反应式避障扩展到策略性长程导航。

Result: 在仿真中，HiPAN相比经典反应式规划器和端到端基线方法实现了更高的导航成功率和路径效率；真实实验验证了其在多种非结构3D环境中的适用性。

Conclusion: HiPAN通过分层后肢自适应导航和课程学习，有效解决了四足机器人在非结构3D环境中的长程导航难题，兼具高成功率和计算效率。

Abstract: Navigating quadruped robots in unstructured 3D environments poses significant challenges, requiring goal-directed motion, effective exploration to escape from local minima, and posture adaptation to traverse narrow, height-constrained spaces. Conventional approaches employ a sequential mapping-planning pipeline but suffer from accumulated perception errors and high computational overhead, restricting their applicability on resource-constrained platforms. To address these challenges, we propose Hierarchical Posture-Adaptive Navigation (HiPAN), a framework that operates directly on onboard depth images at deployment. HiPAN adopts a hierarchical design: a high-level policy generates strategic navigation commands (planar velocity and body posture), which are executed by a low-level, posture-adaptive locomotion controller. To mitigate myopic behaviors and facilitate long-horizon navigation, we introduce Path-Guided Curriculum Learning, which progressively extends the navigation horizon from reactive obstacle avoidance to strategic navigation. In simulation, HiPAN achieves higher navigation success rates and greater path efficiency than classical reactive planners and end-to-end baselines, while real-world experiments further validate its applicability across diverse, unstructured 3D environments.

</details>


### [93] [3D Generation for Embodied AI and Robotic Simulation: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26509)
*Tianwei Ye,Yifan Mao,Minwen Liao,Jian Liu,Chunchao Guo,Dazhao Du,Quanxin Shou,Fangqi Zhu,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了面向具身AI的3D生成技术，将文献组织为三个角色：数据生成器（生成可交互的3D资产）、仿真环境（构建交互式任务场景）和Sim2Real桥梁（支持数字孪生与真实世界迁移），指出该领域正从视觉真实感转向交互就绪性，并识别了物理标注稀缺、几何质量与物理有效性脱节、评估碎片化及仿真到现实鸿沟等关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI和机器人系统对可扩展、多样化、物理真实的3D内容需求日益增长，但现有3D生成技术主要关注视觉真实感，缺乏对交互就绪性（如运动学结构、材质属性、场景交互支持）的考量，因此需要系统性综述来梳理该领域的研究现状与挑战。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，按3D生成在具身系统中扮演的三个角色（数据生成器、仿真环境、Sim2Real桥梁）对文献进行分类组织，并从视觉真实感向交互就绪性的转变趋势进行分析。

Result: 识别出3D生成面向具身AI的三个关键角色，发现领域正从视觉真实感转向交互就绪性，并总结出四大瓶颈：物理标注稀缺、几何质量与物理有效性脱节、评估碎片化、仿真到现实鸿沟。

Conclusion: 3D生成技术要成为具身智能的可靠基础，必须解决物理标注、几何物理一致性、统一评估标准和Sim2Real迁移等核心挑战，向交互就绪性方向发展。

Abstract: Embodied AI and robotic systems increasingly depend on scalable, diverse, and physically grounded 3D content for simulation-based training and real-world deployment. While 3D generative modeling has advanced rapidly, embodied applications impose requirements far beyond visual realism: generated objects must carry kinematic structure and material properties, scenes must support interaction and task execution, and the resulting content must bridge the gap between simulation and reality. This survey presents the first survey of 3D generation for embodied AI and organizes the literature around three roles that 3D generation plays in embodied systems. In \emph{Data Generator}, 3D generation produces simulation-ready objects and assets, including articulated, physically grounded, and deformable content for downstream interaction; in \emph{Simulation Environments}, it constructs interactive and task-oriented worlds, spanning structure-aware, controllable, and agentic scene generation; and in \emph{Sim2Real Bridge}, it supports digital twin reconstruction, data augmentation, and synthetic demonstrations for downstream robot learning and real-world transfer. We also show that the field is shifting from visual realism toward interaction readiness, and we identify the main bottlenecks, including limited physical annotations, the gap between geometric quality and physical validity, fragmented evaluation, and the persistent sim-to-real divide, that must be addressed for 3D generation to become a dependable foundation for embodied intelligence. Our project page is at https://3dgen4robot.github.io.

</details>


### [94] [LLM-Flax : Generalizable Robotic Task Planning via Neuro-Symbolic Approaches with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26569)
*Seongmin Kim,Daegyu Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-Flax框架，利用本地LLM和仅PDDL领域文件，自动生成松弛与互补规则、实现LLM引导的失败恢复以及零样本物体重要性评分，在MazeNamo基准上平均成功率0.945，超越人工基线的0.828。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号任务规划器在新领域部署时需大量人工：领域专家需手动编写松弛和互补规则，且需数百个训练问题来监督GNN物体评分器，耗时耗力。

Method: 提出三阶段框架LLM-Flax：阶段1通过结构化提示与格式验证及自纠错自动生成松弛和互补规则；阶段2引入LLM引导的失败恢复，采用可行性门控预算策略，在每次LLM调用前预留API延迟成本以避免下游松弛回退资源枯竭；阶段3用零样本LLM物体重要性评分替代领域训练的GNN。

Result: 在MazeNamo基准（8个基准，网格10x10、12x12、15x15）上平均SR 0.945 vs 人工基线0.828（+0.117）；12x12 Expert上SR 0.733 vs 人工0.000；15x15 Hard上SR 1.000 vs 人工0.900；阶段3无训练数据下12x12 Hard达SR 0.720。

Conclusion: LLM-Flax完全消除了人工编写规则、训练GNN和监督数据的三种手动工作，显著提升成功率；阶段3可行但面临上下文窗口瓶颈，是未来主要开放挑战。

Abstract: Deploying a neuro-symbolic task planner on a new domain today requires significant manual effort: a domain expert must author relaxation and complementary rules, and hundreds of training problems must be solved to supervise a Graph Neural Network (GNN) object scorer. We propose LLM-Flax, a three-stage framework that eliminates all three sources of manual effort using a locally hosted LLM given only a PDDL domain file. Stage 1 automatically generates relaxation and complementary rules via structured prompting with format validation and self-correction. Stage 2 introduces LLM-guided failure recovery with a feasibility-gated budget policy that explicitly reserves API latency cost before each LLM call, preventing the downstream relaxation fallback from being starved. Stage 3 replaces the domain-trained GNN entirely with zero-shot LLM object importance scoring, requiring no training data. We evaluate all three stages on the MazeNamo benchmark across 10x10, 12x12, and 15x15 grids (8 benchmarks total). LLM-Flax achieves average SR 0.945 versus the manual baseline's 0.828 (+0.117), matching or outperforming manual rules on every one of the eight benchmarks. On 12x12 Expert, LLM-Flax attains SR 0.733 where the manual planner fails entirely (SR 0.000); on 15x15 Hard, it achieves SR 1.000 versus Manual's 0.900. Stage 3 demonstrates feasibility (SR 0.720 on 12x12 Hard with no training data) but faces a context-window bottleneck at scale, pointing to the primary open challenge for future work.

</details>


### [95] [STAR-Filter: Efficient Convex Free-Space Approximation via Starshaped Set Filtering in Noisy Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26626)
*Yuwei Wu,Yichen Zhao,Dexter Ong,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STAR-Filter是一种轻量级框架，通过星形集构造作为快速滤波器，在碰撞规避空间中高效生成凸区域（多面体），显著降低计算开销并对传感器噪声具有鲁棒性，在嘈杂环境下的安全飞行走廊生成和敏捷四旋翼规划中展现出最低计算时间和较低保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于迭代优化膨胀的方法在复杂障碍物环境或传感器数据噪声较大时效率下降，且对初始化和精确几何模型依赖性强，需要一种更高效、鲁棒的凸区域生成方法。

Method: 提出STAR-Filter框架，利用星形集构造作为快速过滤器；通过识别障碍物点作为主动支撑约束，显著减少冗余计算，同时保持可行性和对传感器噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 在仿真中实现了最低计算时间，降低了多面体生成的保守性，在真实噪声和大规模数据上表现优异。

Conclusion: STAR-Filter框架在嘈杂环境下用于安全飞行走廊生成和敏捷四旋翼规划是有效的，兼顾了计算效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Approximating collision-free space is fundamental to robot planning in complex environments. Convex geometric representations, such as polytopes and ellipsoids, are widely employed due to their structural properties, which can be easily integrated with convex optimization. Iterative optimization-based inflation methods can generate large volume polytopes in cluttered environments, but their efficiency degrades as the obstacle set becomes more complex or when sensor data are noisy. These methods are also sensitive to initialization and often rely on accurate geometric models. In this paper, we propose the STAR-Filter, a lightweight framework that employs starshaped set construction as a fast filter for convex region generation in collision-free space. By identifying obstacle points as active supporting constraints, the proposed method significantly reduces redundant computation while preserving feasibility and robustness to sensor noise. We provide theoretical and numerical analyses that characterize the structural properties of the starshaped set and proposed pipeline in environments of varying complexity. Simulation results show that the proposed framework achieves the lowest computation time and reduces conservativeness in polytope generation for real-world noisy and large-scale data. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework for Safe Flight Corridor (SFC) generation and agile quadrotor planning in noisy environments.

</details>


### [96] [ATLAS: An Annotation Tool for Long-horizon Robotic Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26637)
*Sergej Stanovcic,Daniel Sliwowski,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ATLAS是一个专为长时序机器人动作分割设计的标注工具，支持多模态数据（多视角视频和本体感知信号）的时间同步可视化，原生兼容ROS bag和RLDS等机器人数据集格式，并提供键盘中心的高效标注界面。实验表明，相比ELAN，ATLAS将平均每动作标注时间减少至少6%，时间对齐精度提升2.8%以上，边界误差降低五倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标注工具主要为纯视觉数据设计，不支持机器人专用时间序列信号（如夹爪状态、力/力矩）的同步可视化，且适配不同数据集格式需要大量额外工作，难以满足长时序机器人动作分割标注的需求。

Method: ATLAS提供时间同步的多模态数据可视化（多视角视频+本体感知信号），支持动作边界、动作标签和任务结果的标注；通过模块化数据集抽象层原生支持ROS bag和RLDS格式，并可直接扩展至新格式；采用键盘中心化界面以减少标注工作量。

Result: 在接触式装配任务实验中，ATLAS相比ELAN将平均每动作标注时间减少至少6%；相比纯视觉标注工具，加入时间序列数据后时间对齐精度提升2.8%以上，边界误差降低五倍。

Conclusion: ATLAS通过多模态时间同步可视化和高效键盘中心化设计，显著提升了机器人长时序动作分割的标注效率与精度，尤其适用于需要力/力矩等本体感知信号的精细操作任务。

Abstract: Annotating long-horizon robotic demonstrations with precise temporal action boundaries is crucial for training and evaluating action segmentation and manipulation policy learning methods. Existing annotation tools, however, are often limited: they are designed primarily for vision-only data, do not natively support synchronized visualization of robot-specific time-series signals (e.g., gripper state or force/torque), or require substantial effort to adapt to different dataset formats. In this paper, we introduce ATLAS, an annotation tool tailored for long-horizon robotic action segmentation. ATLAS provides time-synchronized visualization of multi-modal robotic data, including multi-view video and proprioceptive signals, and supports annotation of action boundaries, action labels, and task outcomes. The tool natively handles widely used robotics dataset formats such as ROS bags and the Reinforcement Learning Dataset (RLDS) format, and provides direct support for specific datasets such as REASSEMBLE. ATLAS can be easily extended to new formats via a modular dataset abstraction layer. Its keyboard-centric interface minimizes annotation effort and improves efficiency. In experiments on a contact-rich assembly task, ATLAS reduced the average per-action annotation time by at least 6% compared to ELAN, while the inclusion of time-series data improved temporal alignment with expert annotations by more than 2.8% and decreased boundary error fivefold compared to vision-only annotation tools.

</details>


### [97] [Atomic-Probe Governance for Skill Updates in Compositional Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26689)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种配对采样交叉版本交换协议，用于分析机器人技能库更新对组合策略性能的影响。在双轴插销任务中发现“主导技能效应”：一个ECM成功率86.7%而其他≤26.7%，其是否参与组合导致成功率最多±50pp变化。提出原子质量探针和混合选择器（Hybrid Selector）用于技能更新治理，在144次决策中，纯原子探针代价为零但落后完全重验证23pp，混合选择器（m=10）以46%代价将差距缩小至~12pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能组合方法（如BLADE、SymSkill等）假设技能库在测试时冻结，未分析技能替换（版本更新）如何改变组合结果。研究旨在填补这一空白，理解机器人技能库持续更新对组合策略性能的影响。

Method: 提出配对采样交叉版本交换协议；在robosuite上设计多任务实验；发现主导技能效应；测试离策略行为距离作为预测指标的失败；提出原子质量探针和混合选择器（原子探针+选择性组合重验证），绘制Pareto前沿。

Result: 双轴插销任务中发现主导技能效应（86.7% vs ≤26.7%，组合成功率±50pp）；离策略行为距离无法识别主导ECM；纯原子探针代价零但准确率64.6%（vs 完全重验证87.5%）；混合选择器(m=10)以46%代价达~75%；跨任务平均纯原子探针仅落后3pp。

Conclusion: 原子质量探针是首个原则性且可部署的技能更新治理原语；混合选择器在代价和准确性间提供灵活权衡；主导技能效应揭示了技能组合中非对称贡献的关键现象。

Abstract: Skill libraries in deployed robotic systems are continually updated through fine-tuning, fresh demonstrations, or domain adaptation, yet existing typed-composition methods (BLADE, SymSkill, Generative Skill Chaining) treat the library as frozen at test time and do not analyze how composition outcomes change when a skill is replaced. We introduce a paired-sampling cross-version swap protocol on robosuite manipulation tasks to characterize this dimension of compositional skill learning. On a dual-arm peg-in-hole task we discover a dominant-skill effect: one ECM achieves 86.7% atomic success rate while every other ECM is at or below 26.7%, and whether this dominant ECM enters a composition shifts the success rate by up to +50pp. We characterize the boundary on a simpler pick task where all atomic policies saturate at 100% and the effect is undefined. Across three tasks we further find that off-policy behavioral distance metrics fail to identify the dominant ECM, ruling out the natural cheap predictor. We propose an atomic-quality probe and a Hybrid Selector combining per-skill probes (zero per-decision cost) with selective composition revalidation (full cost), and characterize its Pareto frontier on 144 skill-update decisions. On T6 the atomic-only probe sits 23pp below full revalidation (64.6% vs 87.5% oracle match) at zero per-decision cost; a Hybrid Selector with m=10 closes most of that gap to ~12pp at 46% of full-revalidation cost. On the cross-task average over 144 events, atomic-only is within 3pp of full revalidation under a mixed-oracle caveat. The atomic-quality probe is, to our knowledge, the first principled, deployment-ready primitive for skill-update governance in compositional robot policies.

</details>


### [98] [Unified 4D World Action Modeling from Video Priors with Asynchronous Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26694)
*Jun Guo,Qiwei Li,Peiyan Li,Zilong Chen,Nan Sun,Yifei Su,Heyun Wang,Yuan Zhang,Xinghang Li,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-WAM是一个统一的4D世界模型，通过多视图RGB-D视频预测和异步噪声采样，在单一框架中同时实现实时机器人动作执行和高保真4D世界合成，在RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0基准上分别达到79.2%和90.7%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一世界模型（如UWM）仅在2D像素空间建模，无法平衡动作执行效率与世界建模质量，缺乏3D空间信息感知能力。

Method: 1) 利用预训练视频扩散模型的视觉先验，通过预测多视图RGB-D视频来想象未来世界；2) 轻量级结构适配：复制预训练扩散Transformer最后几个块作为专用深度预测分支；3) 异步噪声采样(ANS)：联合优化生成质量和动作解码效率，推理时对不同任务采用不同去噪步数，训练时从联合分布采样对齐推理分布。

Result: 在超过5,800小时的机器人数据上预训练，X-WAM在RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0基准上分别达到79.2%和90.7%的平均成功率，同时在视觉和几何指标上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: X-WAM成功将实时动作执行与高保真4D世界建模统一于单一框架，验证了通过异步噪声采样和轻量级结构适配可以有效解决动作效率与生成质量之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: We propose X-WAM, a Unified 4D World Model that unifies real-time robotic action execution and high-fidelity 4D world synthesis (video + 3D reconstruction) in a single framework, addressing the critical limitations of prior unified world models (e.g., UWM) that only model 2D pixel-space and fail to balance action efficiency and world modeling quality. To leverage the strong visual priors of pretrained video diffusion models, X-WAM imagines the future world by predicting multi-view RGB-D videos, and obtains spatial information efficiently through a lightweight structural adaptation: replicating the final few blocks of the pretrained Diffusion Transformer into a dedicated depth prediction branch for the reconstruction of future spatial information. Moreover, we propose Asynchronous Noise Sampling (ANS) to jointly optimize generation quality and action decoding efficiency. ANS applies a specialized asynchronous denoising schedule during inference, which rapidly decodes actions with fewer steps to enable efficient real-time execution, while dedicating the full sequence of steps to generate high-fidelity video. Rather than entirely decoupling the timesteps during training, ANS samples from their joint distribution to align with the inference distribution. Pretrained on over 5,800 hours of robotic data, X-WAM achieves 79.2% and 90.7% average success rate on RoboCasa and RoboTwin 2.0 benchmarks, while producing high-fidelity 4D reconstruction and generation surpassing existing methods in both visual and geometric metrics.

</details>


### [99] [Rule-based High-Level Coaching for Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning in Search-and-Rescue UAV Missions Under Limited-Simulation Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26833)
*Mahya Ramezani,Holger Voos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层决策框架，将固定规则高层规划器与在线目标条件低层强化学习控制器结合，用于无人机搜索救援任务。在无预训练条件下，高层提供可解释的安全引导，低层通过模式感知优先经验回放在线学习。实验表明该方法能提升早期安全性和样本效率，减少碰撞终止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对无人机搜救场景中模拟训练数据有限、早期部署适应性差的问题，现有方法在安全性与可解释性方面存在不足，亟需一种能在无预训练条件下实现安全在线适应的决策框架。

Method: 1) 固定规则高层规划器：离线从结构化任务规范编译为确定性规则，提供推荐动作、规避动作及仲裁权重；2) 目标条件低层强化学习控制器：在线学习，采用任务定义稠密奖励与模式感知优先经验回放机制，并融合规则派生元数据。

Result: 在电池感知多目标递送和移动目标递送两项任务中，所提方法主要减少了碰撞终止次数，从而显著提升了早期安全性和样本效率，同时保留了在线适应场景动态的能力。

Conclusion: 将规则引导与在线强化学习相结合的分层框架，能有效弥补无预训练条件下无人机早期部署的安全短板，为实际搜救任务提供了一种兼具可解释性和适应性的可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical decision-making framework for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) missions motivated by search-and-rescue (SAR) scenarios under limited simulation training. The framework combines a fixed rule-based high-level advisor with an online goal-conditioned low-level reinforcement learning (RL) controller. To stress-test early adaptation, we also consider a strict no-pretraining deployment regime. The high-level advisor is defined offline from a structured task specification and compiled into deterministic rules. It provides interpretable mission- and safety-aware guidance through recommended actions, avoided actions, and regime-dependent arbitration weights. The low-level controller learns online from task-defined dense rewards and reuses experience through a mode-aware prioritized replay mechanism augmented with rule-derived metadata. We evaluate the framework on two tasks: battery-aware multi-goal delivery and moving-target delivery in obstacle-rich environments. Across both tasks, the proposed method improves early safety and sample efficiency primarily by reducing collision terminations, while preserving the ability to adapt online to scenario-specific dynamics.

</details>


### [100] [Walk With Me: Long-Horizon Social Navigation for Human-Centric Outdoor Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26839)
*Lingfeng Zhang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Xizhou Bu,Yingbo Tang,Hongsheng Li,Jinghui Lu,Xiu-shen Wei,Jiayi Ma,Yu Liu,Jing Zhang,Hangjun Ye,Xiaojun Liang,Long Chen,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Walk with Me 提出了一种无地图框架，通过结合GPS上下文、候选兴趣点、高层次视觉语言模型（VLM）和低层次视觉语言-动作模型（VLA），实现从高级自然语言指令到安全、长视野、社交合规的户外导航行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于地图的方法依赖昂贵的高清预建地图，而学习策略多局限于室内和短视野场景，缺乏在开放世界户外环境中将高级自然语言意图转化为安全、长视野、社交合规导航行为的能力。

Method: 利用GPS上下文和公共地图API的轻量级候选兴趣点进行语义目的地定位和路径点提案；高层次VLM将抽象指令具体化为具体目的地并规划粗略路径点序列；在执行中，通过观测感知路由机制判断低层次VLA是否能处理当前情况，否则触发高层次VLM进行明确安全推理，对拥挤路口等复杂场景执行停等行为。

Result: 实现了无需预建地图的户外长视野社交导航，能够在复杂场景（如拥挤路口）中触发高层次安全推理和停等行为，同时常规路段由低层次VLA高效执行。

Conclusion: 通过语义意图定位、无地图长视野规划、安全感知推理和低层次动作生成的结合，Walk with Me 实现了面向人类协助的实用户外社交导航。

Abstract: Assisting humans in open-world outdoor environments requires robots to translate high-level natural-language intentions into safe, long-horizon, and socially compliant navigation behavior. Existing map-based methods rely on costly pre-built HD maps, while learning-based policies are mostly limited to indoor and short-horizon settings. To bridge this gap, we propose Walk with Me, a map-free framework for long-horizon social navigation from high-level human instructions. Walk with Me leverages GPS context and lightweight candidate points-of-interest from a public map API for semantic destination grounding and waypoint proposal. A High-Level Vision-Language Model grounds abstract instructions into concrete destinations and plans coarse waypoint sequences. During execution, an observation-aware routing mechanism determines whether the Low-Level Vision-Language-Action policy can handle the current situation or whether explicit safety reasoning from the High-Level VLM is needed. Routine segments are executed by the Low-Level VLA, while complex situations such as crowded crossings trigger high-level reasoning and stop-and-wait behavior when unsafe. By combining semantic intent grounding, map-free long-horizon planning, safety-aware reasoning, and low-level action generation, Walk with Me enables practical outdoor social navigation for human-centric assistance.

</details>


### [101] [STARRY: Spatial-Temporal Action-Centric World Modeling for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26848)
*Yuxuan Tian,Yurun Jin,Bin Yu,Yukun Shi,Hao Wu,Chi Harold Liu,Kai Chen,Cong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STARRY是一种世界模型增强的动作生成策略，通过联合去噪未来时空潜在表示和动作序列，并引入几何感知选择性注意力调制，将深度和末端执行器几何信息转化为token对齐权重以进行选择性动作注意力调制，在RoboTwin 2.0上达到93.82%/93.30%成功率，真实世界实验将平均成功率从42.5%提升至70.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略和世界模型增强策略未能充分建模与动作相关的时空交互结构，而机器人操作任务迫切需要推理未来的时空交互。

Method: 提出STARRY策略，核心包括：(1) 联合去噪未来时空潜在表示与动作序列；(2) 几何感知选择性注意力调制，将预测深度和末端执行器几何信息转换为token对齐的权重，用于选择性动作注意力调制。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0上，Clean和Randomized设置下平均成功率分别达93.82%和93.30%；真实世界实验中，相比π₀.₅基线，平均成功率从42.5%提升至70.8%。

Conclusion: 以动作为中心的时空世界建模对于空间-时间要求严格的机器人动作生成是有效的。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation critically requires reasoning about future spatial-temporal interactions, yet existing VLA policies and world-model-enhanced policies do not fully model action-relevant spatial-temporal interaction structure. We propose STARRY, a world-model-enhanced action-generation policy that aligns spatial-temporal prediction with action generation. STARRY jointly denoises future spatial-temporal latents and action sequences, and introduces Geometry-Aware Selective Attention Modulation to convert predicted depth and end-effector geometry into token-aligned weights for selective action-attention modulation. On RoboTwin 2.0, STARRY achieves 93.82% / 93.30% average success under Clean and Randomized settings. Real-world experiments further improve average success from 42.5% to 70.8% over $π_{0.5}$, demonstrating the effectiveness of action-centric spatial-temporal world modeling for spatial-temporally demanding robotic action generation.

</details>


### [102] [Stochastic Entanglement of Deterministic Origami Tentacles For Universal Robotic Gripping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26897)
*Alec Boron,Bokun Zheng,Ziyang Zhou,Noel Naughton,Suyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种肌腱驱动的折纸触手夹持器，通过局部确定性变形编程与全局随机缠结的协同作用，实现对随机形状物体的通用抓取，具有设计简单、驱动单一的优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有折纸机器人夹持器虽具有紧凑体积和机械柔性的优势，但在动态工作环境中稳健抓取随机形状物体往往需要额外的驱动通道和增加控制复杂度。作者旨在通过折纸触手的确定性卷曲变形与多触手间的随机缠结协同，实现简单驱动下的通用物体抓取。

Method: 每个折纸触手由薄Mylar片切割制成，包含用于引导驱动肌腱的孔、控制变形的折痕以及锥形形状。通过设计特征调控触手的收缩、弯曲和扭转变形，实现单肌腱拉动下的确定性卷曲。多个卷曲触手靠近时产生随机缠结（编织、打结），从而抓取随机形状物体。作者集成折纸力学与Cosserat杆理论建立仿真模型，并进行了重力、水下及模拟在轨抓取实验。

Result: 实验验证了折纸触手的卷曲和缠结能力，实现了在重力环境、水下以及模拟在轨条件下的物体抓取。仿真模型能够关联折纸设计、肌腱变形与集体抓取性能。

Conclusion: 这种折纸触手夹持器提供了一种以简单设计和单一驱动实现稳健物体抓取的新策略，具有在复杂和动态环境中应用的潜力。

Abstract: Origami-inspired robotic grippers have shown promising potential for object manipulation tasks due to their compact volume and mechanical flexibility. However, robust capture of objects with random shapes in dynamic working environments often comes at the cost of additional actuation channels and control complexity. Here, we introduce a tendon-driven origami tentacle gripper capable of universal object gripping by exploiting a synergy between local, deterministic deformation programming and global, stochastic entanglements. Each origami tentacle is made by cutting thin Mylar sheets; It features carefully placed holes for routing an actuation tendon, origami creases for controlling the deformation, and a tapered shape. By tailoring these design features, one can prescribe the shrinking, bending, and twisting deformation, eventually creating deterministic coiling with a simple tendon pull. Then, when multiple coiling tentacles are placed in proximity, stochastic entanglement emerges, allowing the tentacles to braid, knot, and grip objects with random shapes. We derived a simulation model by integrating origami mechanics with Cosserat rods to correlate origami design, tendon deformation, and their collective gripping performance. Then, we experimentally tested how these coiling and entangling origami tentacles can grasp objects under gravity and in water. A stow-and-release deployment mechanism was also tested to simulate in-orbit grasping. Overall, the entertaining origami tentacle gripper presents a new strategy for robust object grasping with simple design and actuation.

</details>


### [103] [Bi-Level Optimization for Contact and Motion Planning in Rope-Assisted Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26910)
*Ruben Malacarne,Ioannis Tsikelis,Enrico Mingo Hoffman,Michele Focchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于绳索辅助攀爬机器人在垂直表面运动规划的框架，通过双层优化方案解决混合整数问题，同时选择可行着陆区域并优化控制输入（绳索张力、腿部力和着陆位置），在ALPINE平台上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绳索辅助攀爬机器人在垂直表面上运动时面临复杂的混合整数规划问题，需要同时决策着陆区域选择和控制输入优化，现有方法难以高效处理此类耦合问题。

Method: 采用双层优化框架：外层使用交叉熵方法（Cross-Entropy Method）求解离散的着陆区域选择问题，内层使用基于梯度的非线性优化计算动力学可行的连续运动控制输入。

Result: 在新型攀爬机器人平台ALPINE上进行了验证，能够在多种具有挑战性的地形配置下实现有效的运动规划。

Conclusion: 所提出的双层优化方案能够有效解决绳索辅助攀爬机器人中着陆区域选择与控制输入优化的耦合问题，为复杂垂直表面上的自主攀爬运动提供了可行的规划框架。

Abstract: This paper presents a planning pipeline framework for locomotion in rope-assisted robots climbing vertical surfaces. The proposed framework is formulated as a bi-level optimization scheme that addresses a mixed-integer problem: selecting feasible terrain regions for landing while simultaneously optimizing the control inputs, namely rope tensions and leg forces, and landing location. The outer level of the optimization is solved using the Cross-Entropy Method, while the inner level relies on gradient-based nonlinear optimization to compute dynamically feasible motions. The approach is validated on a novel climbing robot platform, ALPINE, across a variety of challenging terrain configurations.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Observable Neural ODEs for Identifiable Causal Forecasting in Continuous Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26070)
*Jennifer Wendland,Nicolas Freitag,Maik Kschischo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可观测神经ODE（ObsNODEs），通过将控制理论中的可观测性与因果可识别性联系起来，在存在隐藏混杂因素的连续时间序贯决策问题中识别动态治疗效应，并在合成和真实医疗数据上取得优于现有序列模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间序贯决策问题中存在隐藏混杂因素，导致因果推断困难，现有方法缺乏对动态治疗效应的可识别性理论保证。

Method: 1) 理论证明在潜在状态空间模型中，潜在动力学的可观测性是识别动态治疗效应的必要条件；2) 推导连续时间调整公式，通过测量模型、潜在动力学和滤波分布表达潜在结果分布；3) 提出ObsNODEs，即处于可观测规范形式的神经ODE模型，其状态可从观测中重建，支持反事实预测。

Result: 在合成癌症数据、基于MIMIC-IV的半合成数据以及真实脓毒症数据上，ObsNODEs表现优于近期序列模型。

Conclusion: 将控制理论的观测性概念引入因果推断，为连续时间动态治疗效应识别提供了理论基础，且提出的方法在实践中有效。

Abstract: Causal inference in continuous-time sequential decision problems is challenged by hidden confounders. We show that, in latent state-space models with time-varying interventions, observability of the latent dynamics from observed data is necessary for identifying dynamic treatment effects, linking control-theoretic observability to causal identifiability, even when hidden confounders affect both treatments and outcomes. We derive a continuous-time adjustment formula expressing potential outcome distributions under treatment trajectories via the measurement model, latent dynamics, and the filtering distribution over latent states given observed histories. We propose Observable Neural ODEs (ObsNODEs), Neural ODE models in observable normal form for causal forecasting. ObsNODEs learn continuous-time dynamics with states reconstructible from observations, enabling outcome prediction under alternative treatment paths. Experiments on synthetic cancer data, semi-synthetic data based on MIMIC-IV, and real-world sepsis data show strong performance over recent sequence models.

</details>


### [105] [Budget-Constrained Causal Bandits: Bridging Uplift Modeling and Sequential Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26169)
*Abhirami Pillai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预算约束因果Bandit（BCCB）在线框架，将广告效果学习、用户探索和预算控制统一为单进程，在冷启动场景下无需历史数据即可生效，显著优于传统两阶段离线方法（需约10,000条历史数据）和现有在线方法，在Criteo Uplift数据集上表现更稳定（方差低3-5倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统广告预算分配的两阶段离线流程（先估计异质处理效应，再求解约束优化）在历史数据充足时效果良好，但在冷启动场景（新广告活动、新市场、新客户群体）中因缺乏历史数据而失效，亟需一种无需大量历史数据即可同时学习和分配预算的在线方法。

Method: 提出BCCB框架，将三个组件统一为单一顺序决策过程：1）学习个体层面的广告效果；2）探索响应不确定的用户；3）随时间平滑控制预算消耗。采用在线学习范式，逐用户做出处理决策，实现预算约束下的因果Bandit。

Result: 在Criteo Uplift数据集上，离线方法需约10,000条历史观测才能产生可靠结果，而BCCB从第一个用户起即可有效运行；BCCB的运行间性能方差比离线方法低3-5倍；在所有预算水平下，BCCB一致优于标准Thompson Sampling、预算约束Thompson Sampling和贪婪HTE估计等纯在线方法。

Conclusion: BCCB在冷启动场景下具有显著的数据效率优势，且性能更稳健，更适合实际广告活动的预算规划。

Abstract: Treatment allocation under budget constraints is a central challenge in digital advertising: advertisers must decide which users to show ads to while spending a limited budget wisely. The standard approach follows a two-stage offline pipeline - first collect historical data to estimate heterogeneous treatment effects (HTE), then solve a constrained optimization to allocate the budget. This works well with abundant data, but fails in cold-start settings such as new campaigns, new markets, or new customer segments where little historical data exists. We propose Budget-Constrained Causal Bandits (BCCB), an online framework that learns which users respond to ads while simultaneously spending the budget, making treatment decisions one user at a time. BCCB unifies three components into a single sequential process: learning individual-level ad effectiveness, exploring users whose response is uncertain, and pacing the budget over time. We evaluated on the Criteo Uplift dataset, a large-scale advertising dataset from a real randomized controlled trial. Our key finding is a data-efficiency crossover: offline methods require approximately 10,000 historical observations to produce reliable results, while BCCB operates effectively from the very first user. Furthermore, BCCB exhibits 3-5x lower performance variance between runs, making it more practical for real campaign planning. Among purely online methods, BCCB consistently outperforms standard Thompson Sampling, budgeted Thompson Sampling, and greedy HTE estimation across all budget levels tested.

</details>


### [106] [A Multimodal and Explainable Machine Learning Approach to Diagnosing Multi-Class Ejection Fraction from Electrocardiograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25942)
*Catherine Ning,Yu Ma,Cindy Beini Wang,Sean McMahon,Joseph Radojevic,Steven Zweibel,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种多模态机器学习框架，结合12导联心电图时序特征和结构化EHR变量，将左心室射血分数（LVEF）分为四个临床层级（正常>50%、轻度降低40-50%、中度降低30-40%、重度降低<30%）。基于36,784对心电图-超声心动图数据训练的XGBoost模型，在四分类任务中达到0.82-0.95的AUROC，优于单一模态基线，并在时间泛化验证中保持性能稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVEF评估依赖超声心动图，在初级医疗和资源有限的场景中可及性受限，亟需一种更易获取的筛查和分诊工具。

Method: 采用多模态机器学习框架，融合12导联心电图时序特征和结构化EHR变量；使用XGBoost分类器；通过SHAP归因识别最具影响力的特征；基于Hartford HealthCare回顾性数据，训练集含36,784对心电图-超声心动图数据（30,952名门诊患者），并在后续时间段的19,966份心电图上进行时间泛化验证。

Result: 多模态模型在四分类任务中达到AUROC：重度0.95、中度0.92、轻度0.82、正常0.91，优于仅用心电图或仅用EHR的基线模型，且在时间验证中保持性能。

Conclusion: 基于心电图的多模态LVEF分层可作为实用的筛查和分诊辅助工具，在资源有限的情况下优先安排确认性影像检查。

Abstract: Left ventricular ejection fraction (LVEF) assessment depends on echocardiography, limiting access in primary care and resource-constrained settings. We developed a multimodal machine-learning framework that combines engineered 12-lead ECG timeseries features with structured EHR variables to classify LVEF into four clinically used strata: normal (>50%), mildly reduced (40-50%), moderately reduced (30-40%), and severely reduced (<30%). To support model explainability, we identified the most influential ECG and EHR features via SHAP attributions. Using retrospective data from Hartford HealthCare, we trained XGBoost models on 36,784 ECG-echocardiogram pairs from 30,952 outpatients and evaluated temporal generalizability on 19,966 ECGs from a subsequent period. The multimodal model achieved one-vs-rest AUROCs of 0.95 (severe), 0.92 (moderate), 0.82 (mild), and 0.91 (normal), outperforming ECG-only and EHR-only baselines, and maintained performance under temporal validation. This work supports ECG-based, multimodal LVEF stratification as a practical screening and triage aid to prioritize confirmatory imaging where resources are limited.

</details>


### [107] [Electricity price forecasting across Norway's five bidding zones in the post-crisis era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26634)
*My Thi Diem Phan,Trung Tuyen Truong,Hoai Phuong Ha,Dat Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了挪威五个核电区2019-2025年的电力价格预测，发现LightGBM在所有区域表现最优（MAE 1.64-5.74 EUR/MWh），仅依赖滞后价格和日历变量的简单模型即可达到高精度，但外部特征（水库水位、天然气价格）在压力市场状态下对误差分层至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2021-2022能源危机及挪威与欧洲大陆电力市场深度融合导致依赖历史数据的预测模型可靠性下降，而针对挪威所有核电区的统一特征贡献评估基准尚缺失。

Method: 构建2019-2025多模态小时级数据集，评估LightGBM、ARX及多种深度学习模型；采用严格因果测试集、滚动起点回测、留一组特征消融及条件体制分析。

Result: LightGBM在所有区域最优（MAE 1.64-5.74 EUR/MWh）；Ridge ARX在北部区域具有竞争力；仅滞后价格和日历特征即可达到高精度；外部特征在压力市场下对误差分层关键。

Conclusion: 模型可解释性和体制意识对于面临市场结构性变化的决策者具有实际价值。

Abstract: Norway's electricity market is heavily dominated by hydropower, but the 2021--2022 energy crisis and stronger integration with Continental Europe have fundamentally altered price formation, reducing the reliability of forecasting models calibrated on historical data. Despite the critical need for updated models, a unified benchmark evaluating feature contributions across all structurally diverse Norwegian bidding zones remains lacking. Here we present a comprehensive evaluation of electricity price forecasting across all five Norwegian Nord Pool bidding zones. We constructed a multimodal hourly dataset spanning 2019--2025 and evaluated eight forecasting model families including LightGBM, ARX, and advanced deep learning architectures using a strictly causal test set. We implemented robust rolling-origin backtesting, leave-one-group-out feature ablation, and conditional regime analysis to dissect model performance and feature utility. Our results show that LightGBM achieves the best performance in every zone with MAE ranging from 1.64 to 5.74~EUR/MWh, while the ridge ARX model remains a highly competitive linear benchmark in northern zones. Feature ablation reveals that models relying solely on lagged prices and calendar variables achieve high accuracy and often match or exceed full multimodal integration. However, conditional regime analysis demonstrates that external features like reservoir levels and gas prices remain crucial to stratify forecast errors, which consistently increase under stressed market regimes. This highlights the practical value of model interpretability and regime awareness for decision makers facing structural changes in market dynamics.

</details>


### [108] [Open Problems in Frontier AI Risk Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25982)
*Marta Ziosi,Miro Plueckebaum,Stephen Casper,Henry Papadatos,Ze Shen Chin,Peter Slattery,James Gealy,Tim G. J. Rudner,Brian Tse,Ariel Gil,Patricia Paskov,Maximilian Negele,Rokas Gipiškis,Nada Madkour,Vera Lummis,Rupal Jain,Luise Eder,Kristina Fort,Malou C. van Draanen Glismann,Inès Belhadj,Amin Oueslati,Anna K. Wisakanto,Richard Mallah,Koen Holtman,Ranj Zuhdi,Daniel S. Schiff,Jessica Newman,Malcolm Murray,Robert Trager*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地梳理了前沿AI风险管理中的未解决问题，将问题分类为科学/技术共识缺乏、与现有风险管理框架不匹配、以及实施层面的不足，并指明各问题的最佳应对主体，旨在为未来研究和治理提供议程设定参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI技术快速迭代导致缺乏稳定的科学共识，且新兴安全实践与既有风险管理框架存在错位甚至相互削弱，亟需系统性的问题梳理以推动有效治理。

Method: 采用问题导向方法，通过结构化文献综述审视风险管理各阶段（规划、识别、分析、评估、缓解），依据问题类型（共识缺乏、框架错位、实施不足）进行分类，并识别对应的最佳行动主体。

Result: 系统性地识别了前沿AI风险管理各阶段的未解决问题，建立了问题分类体系，并明确了开发者、部署者、监管者、标准机构、研究者及第三方评估者等不同主体应负责解决的问题。

Conclusion: 本文作为议程设定性参考文档（附在线数据库），不提供具体解决方案，而是旨在促进协调、减少重复工作，引导未来研究及治理努力，以推动形成稳健且有意义的共识。

Abstract: Frontier AI both amplifies existing risks and introduces qualitatively novel challenges. Not only is there a notable lack of stable scientific consensus resulting from the rapid pace of technological change, but emerging frontier AI safety practices are often misaligned with, or may undermine, established risk management frameworks. To address these challenges, we systematically surface open problems in frontier AI risk management. Adopting a problem-oriented approach, we examine each stage of the risk management process - risk planning, identification, analysis, evaluation, and mitigation - through a structured review of the literature, identifying unresolved challenges and the actors best positioned to address them. Recognising that different types of open problems call for different responses, we classify open problems according to whether they reflect (a) a lack of scientific or technical consensus, (b) misalignment with, or challenges to, established risk management frameworks, or (c) shortcomings in implementation despite apparent consensus and alignment. By mapping these open problems and identifying the actors best positioned to address them - including developers, deployers, regulators, standards bodies, researchers, and third-party evaluators - this work aims to clarify where progress is needed to enable robust and meaningful consensus on frontier AI risk management.The paper does not propose specific solutions; instead, it provides a problem-oriented, agenda-setting reference document, complemented by a living online repository, intended to support coordination, reduce duplication, and guide future research and governance efforts.

</details>


### [109] [Correcting Performance Estimation Bias in Imbalanced Classification with Minority Subconcepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26024)
*Taylor Maxson,Roberto Corizzo,Yaning Wu,Nathalie Japkowicz,Colin Bellinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预测加权平衡准确率(pBA)，利用多类子概念模型的预测后验概率替代真实子概念标签，在不依赖不可获取的标签下实现对类别内子概念异质性的无偏评估，实验表明pBA在子概念分布不均匀时比未加权指标更稳定、更具可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别级评估会掩盖同一类别内不同子概念间的性能差异，导致平均表现良好的模型在特定子群体上失效。现有不平衡分类评估指标偏向较大的少数子概念，而基于真实子概念标签的效用重加权虽能缓解偏差，但测试时标签通常不可获取。

Method: 提出预测加权平衡准确率(pBA)，用多类子概念模型预测的后验概率替代不可用的子概念真实标签；评估权重定义为该后验下的期望效用，生成软性的、具有不确定性感知的度量指标。

Result: 在表格基准、医学影像和文本数据集上，未加权得分在类别内异质性存在时会产生误导，而pBA在子概念分布不均匀但非病态的情况下提供更稳定和可解释的评估结果。

Conclusion: pBA是一种实用的效用加权评估方法，能在子概念标签缺失时有效纠正评估偏差，提高对类内异质性数据的评估可靠性。

Abstract: Class-level evaluation can conceal substantial performance disparities across subconcepts within the same class, causing models that perform well on average to fail on specific subpopulations. Prior work has shown that common evaluation measures for imbalanced classification are biased toward larger minority subconcepts and that utility-based reweighting using true subconcept labels can mitigate this bias; however, such labels are rarely available at test time. We introduce a practical utility-weighted evaluation that replaces unavailable subconcept labels with predicted posterior probabilities from a multiclass subconcept model. Evaluation weights are defined as the expected utility under this posterior, yielding a soft, uncertainty-aware metric we call predicted-weighted balanced accuracy (pBA). Experiments on tabular benchmarks as well as medical-imaging and text datasets show that unweighted scores can be misleading under within-class heterogeneity, while pBA provides more stable and interpretable assessments when subconcept distributions are uneven but not pathological. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/correcting-bias-imbalance-9C6C/.

</details>


### [110] [RaMP: Runtime-Aware Megakernel Polymorphism for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26039)
*Vyom Sharma,Debajyoti Datta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RaMP是一个路由感知的MoE推理调度框架，通过性能区域分析和四参数波成本模型，根据专家路由分布动态选择最优内核配置，在无需修改源码的情况下实现1.14x-1.41x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE推理系统仅根据batch size进行内核调度，忽略了专家路由分布对最优内核配置的影响，导致10-70%的内核吞吐量未被充分利用。

Method: 提出RaMP框架：(1) 性能区域分析，仅从硬件常数推导各优化策略的适用条件；(2) 四参数波成本模型，基于运行时专家直方图预测最优配置，仅需10-24分钟一次性预配置；(3) 协同设计的CuTe DSL内核，暴露134-268个多态配置。

Result: 准确预测全部8个测试架构（含3个未见架构）；平均遗憾率0.93%；Alpha-MoE无需修改源码实现1.14x加速；内核加速1.22x；vLLM端到端加速：相比Triton 1.30x，相比DeepGEMM 1.41x，相比FlashInfer CUTLASS 1.13x。

Conclusion: 路由感知的调度策略能有效释放MoE推理中未被充分利用的性能潜力，且该方法具有架构通用性和内核无关性。

Abstract: The optimal kernel configuration for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference depends on both batch size and the expert routing distribution, yet production systems dispatch from batch size alone, leaving 10-70% of kernel throughput unrealized. We present RaMP, a routing-aware dispatch framework. A performance-region analysis derives, from hardware constants alone, when each optimization helps, correctly predicting all 8 tested architectures, including 3 unseen. A four-parameter wave cost model selects the fastest configuration from the runtime expert histogram, achieving 0.93% mean regret versus exhaustive search, fitted from just 10-24 minutes of one-time profiling per model. Because the model depends only on CTA grid geometry, it is kernel-agnostic: applied to Alpha-MoE, it delivers 1.14x with no source modification. Paired with a co-designed CuTe DSL kernel exposing 134-268 polymorphic configurations, RaMP delivers 1.22x kernel speedup over static dispatch and 1.30x end-to-end speedup in vLLM serving over Triton, 1.41x over DeepGEMM, and 1.13x over FlashInfer CUTLASS.

</details>
